I mean… I haven’t had anything like that, as far as I remember.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel as if someone is putting thoughts into your mind or controlling your actions?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about putting thoughts in my head. But… it does feel like people are watching what I do. The hospital people, and some people from the agency… watching the house, my cleaning routes. Like they’re checking if I’m taking care of my husband right.\n\nDoctor: Have you found yourself losing interest in things you used to care about?\n\nPatient: I don’t know… maybe a little, but I still do what needs doing. I haven’t really thought about it much.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever hear voices talking to each other about you?\n\nPatient: Not really talking to each other… but sometimes, when the house is quiet, I hear a woman’s voice by the kitchen window. She’ll tell me to check the locks, or move his pills. It sounds… close, like she’s right there.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming less motivated or emotionally flat over the past year?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I still get up and do my work, take care of what I have to. I don’t really think about whether I’m “flat” or not.\n\nDoctor: Have other people told you that your speech is hard to follow or that you jump from one idea to another?\n\nPatient: Sometimes, yes. People say I’m hard to follow. I’ll start trying to explain something and it goes from my husband’s heart pills, to the church schedule, to numbers on my client list… and I guess it doesn’t come out right.\n\nDoctor: During this time, have you had a period of feeling extremely high or unusually energetic?\n\nPatient: I don’t know if I’d call it “high.” But… yes, I get going real early, before daylight sometimes. Cleaning, cooking, making lists, calling about appointments. I’ll take on things for church too, even when people tell me I need to slow down.\n\nDoctor: During these times when you get going very early and take on a lot, do you also feel like you need much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes… I guess so. Some nights I only sleep two or three hours, and then I’m up like it’s nothing, wanting to scrub floors or sort papers. I don’t feel as tired as I should.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you heard that woman’s voice even when you were not in one of those high-energy, low-sleep periods?\n\nPatient: Yes… there was a time like that. I wasn’t so wound up then, I was sleeping more, for a few weeks I think. But I still heard her voice by the kitchen window… and I still felt like the agency was watching us.\n\nDoctor: During the past year, have you had a period when you felt depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: I’m… not really sure. I have bad days, but I don’t know if it was most of the day, nearly every day. I don’t keep track like that.\n\nDoctor: Was there ever a time when the voice and the feeling of being watched continued for at least two weeks when you were not feeling especially high or unusually energetic?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think so. There was a quieter stretch, a few weeks maybe, where I was sleeping more and not rushing around so much. But I still heard her by the kitchen window, and I still thought the agency was watching us.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did you feel especially powerful or that you had unusual abilities?\n\nPatient: Yes… I guess I did. I don’t really notice when I’m talking more.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did you spend much more money than usual?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t think I did, not much anyway. When the house is quiet, I hear a woman’s voice… usually by the kitchen window. She’ll tell me to check the locks, or move my husband’s pills. I still get up and do my work, take care of what I have to. Cleaning, cooking, making lists, calling about appointments. I’ll take on things for church too, even when people tell me I need to slow down.\n\nDoctor: During these times when you get going very early and take on a lot, do you also feel like you need much less sleep than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes… I guess so. Some nights I only sleep two or three hours, and then I’m up like it’s nothing, wanting to scrub floors or sort papers. I have bad days, but I don’t know if it was most of the day, nearly every day. I don’t keep track like that.\n\nDoctor: Was there ever a time when the voice and the feeling of being watched continued for at least two weeks when you were not feeling especially high or unusually energetic?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think so. There was a quieter stretch, a few weeks maybe, where I was sleeping more and not rushing around so much. But I still heard her by the kitchen window, and I still thought the agency was watching us.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did you feel especially powerful or that you had unusual abilities?\n\nPatient: Yes… I guess I did. I felt like I was the only one who had the sense, or the strength, to keep my husband and the house safe… and maybe some people from church too. I don’t really notice when I’m talking more.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did you spend much more money than usual?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure. I don’t think I did, not much anyway. I usually don’t have a lot extra to spend, so… I’d have to think about it.\n\nDoctor: During those high-energy times, did you feel more important or specially chosen than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes… I did feel that way some. Like I was the only one who really understood what was coming, and that I had to keep my husband, my home, and some people at church safe. I felt like I was the only one who had the sense and strength to keep my husband, my home, and even some church people safe from what was coming.\n\nDoctor: Was there ever a time when you heard the woman’s voice or felt watched even though you were not feeling especially up, down, or unusually energetic?\n\nPatient: Yes… there was a quieter stretch, a few weeks maybe. I was sleeping more and not running around so much, but I still heard that woman’s voice by the kitchen window. And I still felt like the agency was watching us."
